## SV-10043 — Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10043 · Epic · **In Progress** · parent None · reporter Sasha Grosman · assignee Chris Ward · created 2026-09-15T00:29 · updated 2026-10-14T07:54 · resolution None
Links: implements PRD-2; relates to SV-11087; relates to SV-11235; relates to SV-11279

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Problem Statement

Dispatchers, foremen, and shop managers must scan one long Work Orders list and open individual work orders to understand technician load or change the lead technician. This makes daily workload balancing slow and hides unassigned work.

### Goal

Give shops List, grouped Tech View, and technician-based Board View displays with remembered preferences and safe lead reassignment, while preserving existing filters, permissions, line work, and time data.

### Scope

#### Included

* 1. Switch between display options — Let users switch between List, Tech View, and Board View while keeping their current work and personal display state.
  
* 1. Tech View — table grouped by lead technician — Group the Work Orders table by lead technician so dispatchers can scan each technician's workload without losing table detail.
  
* 1. Board View — board by lead technician — Show work orders on a board with one column per lead technician, with fixed Unassigned, sticky headers, and shared pins.
  
* 1. Reassign or unassign the lead technician from the board or grouped table — Let authorized users assign, reassign, or remove a lead technician while preserving line work, audit history, and time data.
  
* 1. Choose what is shown — Fields to display (Board View) and columns (tables) — Let users choose the information shown in table columns and Board View cards, with safe defaults and financial-field permissions.
  
* 1. Density in Tech View and Board View — Let users choose a shared Compact, Regular, or Comfortable density in Tech View and Board View. List keeps today's row size.
  
* 1. Show line technicians on cards and rows — Show the lead and distinct line technicians as a compact, discoverable avatar group on work order cards and rows. On the Lines tab, a line with no labor technician shows its scheduled technicians instead of “Unassigned” (S7-R8 to S7-R10).
  
* 1. Remove the tech story check-mark indicator — Remove the unexplained tech-story check mark without changing tech-story entry, editing, or requiredness.
  
* 1. Drag to reorder work and technician groups/columns — Let users reorder work and technician groups, and reassign work by dragging between technicians.
  
* 1. Keyboard access and focus — design questions to resolve — Define keyboard and focus behavior for cards, rows, actions, pinning, collapsing, and drag alternatives.
  
* 1. Google Analytics for display and field usage — Measure display, field, and density usage so future defaults can be based on observed behavior.
  

#### Deferred

* Board View columns by work order status.
* Work-order-level assignment to multiple technicians.
* Parts-state indicators on Board View cards.

### Key Decisions

* List remains the default display and stays exactly as in production; the only addition to the Work Orders page is the display switcher. No feature flag.
* The switcher follows the List's screen-width switch: narrower than 1024px shows today's List with no switcher; at 1024px and wider Tech View and Board View are offered on any device (S1-R14).
* Tech View and Board View group by lead technician; Unassigned remains fixed first.
* Work Orders view permission controls visibility and technician-only ordering; dragging any work order or changing the lead needs Work Orders create and edit (S4-N1).
* The order of work orders is saved per location, so everyone at the location sees the same order in Tech View and Board View (S9-R11). Technician group/column order and up to three pins are saved per user; pins are shared by Tech View and Board View (S9-R12, S3-R17).
* Density is shared by Tech View and Board View; List keeps today's row size (S6-R3, S6-E1).
* Lead changes preserve explicit line assignments, completed work, logged labor, active clock-ins, status, and time according to the line-movement table.
* Changing or removing the lead in Board View or Tech View (drag or Reassign dialog) asks whether to clear the outgoing lead's whole-work-order shifts, only when one has not ended. No prompt on the work order page or from List, as today. Decided 2026-10-06 (S4-R11, S4-R26, S4-R28 to S4-R31).
* Clearing removes shifts that haven't started and ends a running shift at the moment of the change; ended shifts, line-level shifts and recorded time stay. The lead change and the clearing succeed or fail together (S4-R25, S4-R27).
* The technician picker is the assignment-eligibility boundary. Prohibited work order statuses are enforced on every lead-change path.
* Deactivating a technician does not change work orders they already lead; they just receive no new assignments.

### Validation and release

* V-1: before acceptance, test load times and UI behavior with production-scale volumes (Foothills Group, Haylock Truck and Trailer).
* V-2: test a one-to-two-person shop on desktop, including empty and overflow states.
* R-1: the release note tells shops, in plain language, about the four changes to how the lead technician works today, including on the work order page.

### Related Issues

* [SV-8032](https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-8032) — eligible roles for lead assignment; addressed by technician eligibility requirements.
* [SV-7605](https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-7605) — asks Schedule removal to clear the lead, conflicting with the current spec decision that Schedule and lead changes are independent.

### Stories (ordered)

* **Story 1: Switch between display options** — Let users switch between List, Tech View, and Board View while keeping their current work and personal display state.
* **Story 2: Tech View — table grouped by lead technician** — Group the Work Orders table by lead technician so dispatchers can scan each technician's workload without losing table detail.
* **Story 3: Board View — board by lead technician** — Show work orders on a board with one column per lead technician, with fixed Unassigned, sticky headers, and shared pins.
* **Story 4: Reassign or unassign the lead technician from the board or grouped table** — Let authorized users assign, reassign, or remove a lead technician while preserving line work, audit history, and time data.
* **Story 5: Choose what is shown — Fields to display (Board View) and columns (tables)** — Let users choose the information shown in table columns and Board View cards, with safe defaults and financial-field permissions.
* **Story 6: Density in Tech View and Board View** — Let users choose a shared Compact, Regular, or Comfortable density in Tech View and Board View. List keeps today's row size.
* **Story 7: Show line technicians on cards and rows** — Show the lead and distinct line technicians as a compact, discoverable avatar group on work order cards and rows. On the Lines tab, a line with no labor technician shows its scheduled technicians instead of “Unassigned” (S7-R8 to S7-R10).
* **Story 8: Remove the tech story check-mark indicator** — Remove the unexplained tech-story check mark without changing tech-story entry, editing, or requiredness.
* **Story 9: Drag to reorder work and technician groups/columns** — Let users reorder work and technician groups, and reassign work by dragging between technicians.
* **Story 11: Keyboard access and focus — design questions to resolve** — Define keyboard and focus behavior for cards, rows, actions, pinning, collapsing, and drag alternatives.
* **Story 12: Google Analytics for display and field usage** — Measure display, field, and density usage so future defaults can be based on observed behavior.

---

### Source

[Product specification](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) · [Review decisions and open questions](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=853901313)

_Updated 2026-10-08 to match PRD v42: Story 10 removed; density only in Tech View and Board View; shift prompt decided; per-location work-order order; V-1, V-2, R-1 added._

Each column header shows the technician’s name, avatar, and a count of the work orders currently shown in that column, after the filter view and filters are applied, so the count always matches the cards listed. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R12](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+3. The Unassigned column is the first column and is fixed: it stays in place while the user scrolls the technician columns horizontally. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+4. Column headers (technician names and counts) stay pinned at the top of the board while the user scrolls vertically, so long columns never hide whose column is being viewed. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R3](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+5. Board View cards include the mandatory fields specified in S5-R3. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R4](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+6. Keep each card’s status badge in a fixed position. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R5](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+7. Reveal the card’s more-actions overlay on hover or keyboard focus. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R13](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+8. Include Reassign lead technician in the card’s more-actions menu. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R14](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+9. Clicking a card (outside the more-actions control) opens the work order. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R6](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+10. Allow users to pin a technician from the column header. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R7](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+11. Place pinned technicians after fixed Unassigned and before unpinned technicians. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R15](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+12. Append each newly pinned technician after the existing pins. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R16](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+13. Share the same saved pin selection between Tech View and Board View. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R17](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+14. Persist pins per user across sessions, logout/login, and devices. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R18](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+15. At three pinned technicians, disable other pin controls with: "You can pin up to 3 technicians." — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R8](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+16. Unpinning frees a slot in both Tech View and Board View. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R19](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+17. Keep empty eligible technician columns visible as drop targets, except while Assigned to me is enabled (S3-N1), subject to S1-N1. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R9](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+18. Keep the Unassigned column visible even when it has no work, showing its empty state, so work orders can be dragged to it. +19. A technician column with no work orders under the current filters, and an empty Unassigned column, show "Drag a work order here to assign it" to a user who can reassign work orders (S4-N1), and "No work orders" to everyone else. — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R20](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+20. The empty column of a pinned technician who has been deactivated shows "No work orders" to every user, because nothing can be dropped there (S4-N5). — ([PRD: Story 3, S3-R20a](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-3%3A-Board-View-%E2%80%94-board-by-lead-technician))
+21. A pinned technician who is not enrolled at this location gets no column here. Removing the lead technician (moving the work order to Unassigned) shows the same shift-clearing prompt as changing it (S4-R11). — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R26](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+25. The lead change and the shift clearing succeed or fail together. If clearing fails, the lead technician is not changed and S4-N8 shows. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R27](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+26. The shift-clearing prompt (S4-R11, S4-R26) shows only when the outgoing lead has a work-order-level shift on this work order that has not ended. A work-order-level shift is one that covers every current line of the work order, which is what the Schedule’s Entire work order drop creates. A shift on only some lines does not show the prompt. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R28](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+27. The prompt reads: title "Clear {Name}'s scheduled shifts?" (with no name: "Clear the lead technician's scheduled shifts?"); text "{Name} has shifts scheduled for this whole work order. Clearing removes shifts that haven't started and ends one that's under way now. Shifts on individual lines and recorded time don't change."; buttons "Keep shifts", "Clear shifts" and "Cancel". — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R29](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+28. Cancel, or closing the prompt, calls the change off. A dragged card goes back to where it was; in the Reassign dialog the user is back in the dialog with their choice kept. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R30](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+29. The prompt shows on Board View and Tech View, both when dragging and in the Reassign dialog. Changing the lead technician on the work order page or from the List does not show it, as today. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-R31](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+30. Require Work Orders create and edit permission to drag work orders or access Reassign lead technician. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+31. Do not allow the lead technician to change in Invoiced or Paid status. An Invoiced or Paid work order can still be dragged to reorder it within its current technician (or within Unassigned), but it cannot be moved to another technician, to Unassigned, or out of Unassigned. Declined and Complete work orders can be moved freely, as today. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+32. The disabled Reassign lead technician action on an Invoiced or Paid work order shows this tooltip: "The lead technician can’t be changed once a work order is Invoiced or Paid." — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N6](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+33. Visually indicate that an Invoiced or Paid work order can only be reordered within its current technician, not moved to another one. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N7](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+34. If reassignment fails, return the card or row to its original column or group. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N3](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+35. Failed reassignment shows a user-dismissible alert: "Failed to update lead technician, please try again." — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N8](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+36. A reorder within one technician that cannot be saved shows the alert "Couldn't save the new order. Please try again." and the card goes back to where it was. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N10](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+37. A card dropped next to a work order that someone else has just moved is not saved. The alert reads "The card you dropped this next to has moved. Refresh the board and try again." and the card goes back to where it was. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N11](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+38. When two people drop into the same technician at the same moment and the second cannot be saved in time, the alert reads "The board is busy with another move. Please try again." and the card goes back to where it was. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N12](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+39. Selecting the current lead again leaves all assignments unchanged and produces no reassignment toast. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N4](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+40. Nothing can be dropped onto an inactive technician's column/group (S2-N2, S3-N2). — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-N5](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+41. Allow lead changes while the outgoing lead is clocked into a work order line. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+42. An actively clocked-in line retains its assigned technician, even if assignment is implicit or no earlier labor exists. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E6](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+43. When two users reassign the same work order, the later change wins. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+44. After concurrent reassignment, both users see the final lead after refresh. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E7](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+45. If status becomes prohibited during a drag, reject the drop using S4-N3 and S4-N8, with a status explanation. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E3](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+46. The Reassign dialog allows destinations outside the current filter. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E4](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+47. After reassignment, remove the work order from results if it no longer matches the filter. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E8](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
+48. For mixed line assignments, apply S4-R8 independently to each line. — ([PRD: Story 4, S4-E5](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-4%3A-Reassign-or-unassign-the-lead-technician-from-the-board-or-grouped-table))
@@ -61,0 +71,6 @@
+_Context note (S4-R8, SV-11128): "Explicitly assigned" means the line's own technician, set through Edit Line. A technician who is on a line only because of a Schedule shift, whether the shift was dropped on that line or on the entire work order, does not count: the line follows the incoming lead like any other line, and the shift technician stays on the line's technician list. This is how a lead technician change works on the work order screen today._
+
+- Removing the lead technician follows the same table: lines that were following the outgoing lead return to unassigned, and every other line is left as it is.
+- Selecting the technician who is already the lead makes no assignment change (S4-N4).
+- Acceptance criteria must cover all six rows for assign, reassign and remove. Any No condition overrides a Yes condition. Explicitly test active clock-in with zero previously logged labor as well as Complete and logged-labor exclusions.
+
@@ -65 +80 @@
-- [ ] Given each work order line state in the spec table, when the lead changes, then that line moves or stays exactly as the table requires; any No condition overrides Yes. (S4-R8, S4-E1, S4-E5–E6)
+- [ ] Given each work order line state in the spec table, when the lead is assigned, reassigned or removed, then that line moves (on removal, returns to unassigned) or stays exactly as the table requires (any No condition overrides Yes), including an active clock-in with zero logged labor, a Complete line and a line with logged labor; a technician who is on a line only through a Schedule shift does not keep it. (S4-R8, S4-E1, S4-E5–E6)
@@ -68 +83,2 @@
-- [ ] Given a reassignment failure, when the response returns, then the card or row returns to its origin and the dismissible error appears. (S4-N3, S4-N8)
+- [ ] Given a reassignment failure, including a failed shift clearing, when the response returns, then the lead is unchanged, the card or row returns to its origin and the dismissible error appears. (S4-R27, S4-N3, S4-N8)
+- [ ] Given a reorder within one technician that cannot be saved, when the save fails, then the card goes back to where it was and the S4-N10, S4-N11 or S4-N12 alert shows. (S4-N10–N12)
@@ -74 +90 @@
-- [ ] Given a Declined work order, when the user changes its lead by drag or dialog, then the change succeeds as it does today. Given an Invoiced or Paid work order, when the user drags it within its current technician, then it reorders; when they drag it to another technician or to or from Unassigned, then the move is refused. (S4-N2, S4-N7)
+- [ ] Given a Declined or Complete work order, when the user changes its lead by drag or dialog, then the change succeeds as it does today. Given an Invoiced or Paid work order, when the user drags it within its current technician, then it reorders; when they drag it to another technician or to or from Unassigned, then the move is refused. (S4-N2, S4-N7)
@@ -75,0 +92,2 @@
+- [ ] Given a lead change or removal in Board View or Tech View, by drag or the Reassign dialog, when the outgoing lead has a work-order-level shift on this work order that has not ended, then the prompt shows with the S4-R29 title, text and buttons; otherwise, and on the work order page or List, it does not show. (S4-R11, S4-R26, S4-R28–R29, S4-R31)
+- [ ] Given the shift-clearing prompt, when the user chooses Cancel or closes it, then the lead is unchanged; a dragged card goes back to where it was, and in the Reassign dialog the user is back in the dialog with their choice kept. (S4-R30)
@@ -81 +99 @@
-### Open design decisions
+### Design decisions
@@ -83 +101,7 @@
-FF-4 and UX-21 track prompt timing, cancellation, empty and failure states, and notification details. Whether lead removal uses the same prompt remains unanswered. See [Review decisions and open questions](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=853901313).
+The shift-clearing prompt’s wording and behavior are decided in the PRD (S4-R28 to S4-R31, 2026-10-06); UX-21 no longer waits on design. See [Review decisions and open questions](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=853901313).
+
+_Updated 2026-10-08 to match PRD v42: shift-clearing prompt rules (S4-R26–R31), Imported tooltip, reorder failure alerts, Complete in N open, removal line rules._

+_Updated 2026-10-12 to match PRD v47: S4-R8 context note (Schedule shifts never keep a line, SV-11128); S4-R28 defines a work-order-level shift (SV-11126)._
+
+_Updated 2026-10-13 to match PRD v48: S4-N2 locks Invoiced and Paid only; S4-N9 (the Imported tooltip) and its acceptance criterion removed. Imported work orders open in List only (S1-R13a, S1-R13b), so Board View and Tech View never show them._

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10049 · Story · **Ready for QA** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Sasha Grosman · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-09-15T00:34 · updated 2026-10-13T11:53 · resolution None

+8. If saved density cannot be loaded, use Regular. — ([PRD: Story 6, S6-N2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-6%3A-Density-in-Tech-View-and-Board-View))
+9. Density applies to Tech View rows and Board View cards on Work Orders. List keeps today’s row size, and other application tables are unchanged. — ([PRD: Story 6, S6-E1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-6%3A-Density-in-Tech-View-and-Board-View))
@@ -23,3 +23,3 @@
-- [ ] Given any Work Orders display, when Density opens, then Compact, Regular, and Comfortable are available and Regular is the default. (S6-R1, S6-R5)
-- [ ] Given the user changes density, when any Work Orders display renders, then spacing and element size change without altering fields or values. (S6-R2, S6-R6)
-- [ ] Given a saved density, when the user switches views, sessions, devices, or locations, then the same density is restored. (S6-R3, S6-R7)
+- [ ] Given Tech View or Board View, when Density opens, then Compact, Regular, and Comfortable are available and Regular is the default. (S6-R1, S6-R5)
+- [ ] Given the user changes density, when Tech View or Board View renders, then spacing and element size change without altering fields, columns, or values. (S6-R2, S6-R6)
+- [ ] Given a saved density, when the user switches between Tech View and Board View, or changes session, device, or location, then the same density is restored. (S6-R3, S6-R7)
@@ -26,0 +27,2 @@
+- [ ] Given the saved density cannot be loaded, when Tech View or Board View opens, then Regular is used. (S6-N2)
+- [ ] Given List, when it renders, then it has no Density control and keeps today's row size; other application tables are unchanged. (S6-E1)
@@ -30,0 +33,2 @@

+26. Filtered-order examples (confirmed): full order A, B, C, D; the filter shows A, C. Move C immediately above A: clearing the filter yields C, A, B, D. Applies to work orders within a technician and to technician groups/columns, subject to fixed Unassigned and pinned-area boundaries. — ([PRD: Story 9, S9-E2/S9-E3 examples](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/PRD#Story-9%3A-Drag-to-reorder-work-and-technician-groups%2Fcolumns))

(S4-N10–N12, S9-R11)

## SV-10053 — More-actions menu and lead assignment from List

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10053 · Story · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Sasha Grosman · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-09-15T00:34 · updated 2026-10-13T11:53 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10224; relates to SV-10228

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Summary

Add lead assignment and removal to List through the same reassignment flow used in Tech View and Board View.

### Context

This story implements Story 10 of the approved Work Orders display-options spec. Existing Work Orders permissions and current production behavior remain in force unless a listed requirement changes them.

### Requirements

1. Add a more-actions menu to List rows. — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-R1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))
2. List’s Reassign lead technician action opens the dialog specified in Story 4. — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-R2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))
3. Apply Story 4’s reassignment rules to List, except its Board View/Tech View shift-clearing prompt. — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-R3](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))
4. Apply Story 4’s permission and status restrictions to the List action. — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-N1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))
5. Apply Story 4’s cancellation and failure behavior to the List dialog. — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-N2](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))
6. If reassignment removes a work order from List results, remove its row and retain the success toast (S4-E8, S4-R5). — ([PRD: Story 10, S10-E1](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Kanban+Tech+View+Display+Options+Draft+Spec#Story-10%3A-More-actions-menu-and-lead-assignment-from-List))

### Acceptance Criteria

* \[ \] Given List, when a user opens a row's more-actions menu, then Reassign lead technician is available under Story 4's permission and status rules. (S10-R1–R3, S10-N1)
* \[ \] Given the List reassignment dialog, when a user confirms, cancels, or encounters failure, then Story 4's assignment, feedback, audit, and recovery behavior applies. (S10-R2–R3, S10-N2)
* \[ \] Given reassignment causes a work order to stop matching the current filter, when it succeeds, then the row disappears and the success toast remains visible. (S10-E1)

### UI/UX

[Claude Design — Work Orders](https://claude.ai/design/p/787fef1a-9523-43cc-bd06-f88765051d21?file=Work+Orders.dc.html&via=share)

* \[ \] Two rows with the same customer and date keep a stable order across pages

## SV-10222 — Verify Phase 3 — Lead-change hardening

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10222 · Task · **TESTING QA** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Milan Zivanovic · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-09-18T06:16 · updated 2026-10-13T03:13 · resolution None
Links: relates to SV-10047

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### What to verify

Phase 3 of the "Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options" technical plan. Ask Milan Zivanovic for the plan document. Every path that changes a lead technician now goes through one place: status lock, line movement, optional shift clearing and a single audit entry, in one transaction.

Spec: [Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Board+View+Tech+View+Display+Options) · Epic: SV-10043

### Requirements covered

S4-R6, S4-R8, S4-R9, S4-R10, S4-R17 to S4-R25, S4-R27, S4-N2, S4-N4, S4-E1, S4-E2, S4-E5, S4-E6, NFR-008

### Verification checklist

* \[ \] Static gates green
* \[ \] Migration runs, then `doctrine:migrations:diff --allow-empty-diff` reports "No changes detected"
* \[ \] `bin/smoke-test.sh` — no 500s
* \[ \] Change the lead on the work order page (allowed status) by assigning a first lead, reassigning, and removing it, and check all six S4-R8 line rows for each: unassigned lines and lines that followed the old lead move (on removal, lines that followed the old lead go back to unassigned); lines explicitly assigned to someone else, Complete lines, lines with logged time and lines with an active clock-in do not, including a line that follows the lead with an active clock-in and zero logged time. A "No" row always wins over a "Yes"
* \[ \] Reassigning while a technician is clocked in leaves their clock and their line assignment intact
* \[ \] Invoiced and Paid refuse the change on every path, including a direct API call; Declined and Complete can still be changed, as today
* \[ \] The asset-on-site toggle still saves on an invoiced work order
* \[ \] Re-selecting the current lead changes nothing and writes no history entry
* \[ \] Exactly one history entry per lead change, with the acting user
* \[ \] Changing a technician directly on a line still writes its own line history
* \[ \] Moving, resizing or reassigning a shift on the Schedule never changes the lead, as today (S4-R19); nor does creating or deleting one
* \[ \] A lead change, with or without shift clearing, leaves the work order status and all active, recorded and locked time unchanged (S4-R20, S4-R21)
* \[ \] Shift clearing touches only the outgoing lead's whole-work-order shifts: an ended shift stays, a shift not started yet is removed, and a shift under way is ended at the moment of the change (8:00–12:00 changed at 10:00 becomes 8:00–10:00); line-level shifts and other people's shifts are untouched
* \[ \] Choosing to keep shifts changes the lead and leaves every shift as it is (S4-R23). When clearing is chosen, the lead change and the clearing succeed or fail together: if clearing fails, the lead is not changed and the S4-N8 alert shows (S4-R27)
* \[ \] Any bug found is fixed or raised as its own ticket before closing

### Done means

All verification tickets on this epic Done = the feature is ready for QA.

* \[ \] With a preference save failing: the previously saved value stays saved, and switching displays and saving again still work (S1-N3, S1-N4)
* \[ \] Changing a layout setting during a shared-link visit still saves; the link's tab and filters do not
* \[ \] Without "see financial data": no dollar columns or fields are offered, or restored from a saved preference
* \[ \] On a screen narrower than 1024px: today's List exactly (S1-R14), no switcher, density or pickers; the desktop choice is untouched. At 1024px and wider, including a tablet held sideways, Tech View and Board View are offered
* \[ \] Any bug found is fixed or raised as its own ticket before closing

### Done means

All verification tickets on this epic Done = the feature is ready for QA.

Phase 9 of the "Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options" technical plan. Ask Milan Zivanovic for the plan document. **This is the phase where Tech View and Board View become selectable in the display switcher**, so it is also the first time users see the feature.

Spec: [Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Board+View+Tech+View+Display+Options) · Epic: SV-10043

### Requirements covered

S1-R1, S1-R3, S1-R13a, S1-R13b, S1-R14, S2-R8, S9-R1 to S9-R15, S9-N1 to S9-N3, S9-E1 to S9-E3, S4-R1, S4-R7, S4-R11, S4-R25 to S4-R31, S4-N2, S4-N3, S4-N5, S4-N7, S4-N10 to S4-N12, S4-E3, S11-R5, NFR-011

### Verification checklist

* \[ \] Static gates green; `bin/smoke-test.sh` — no 500s
* \[ \] All three display options appear in the switcher; switching keeps the filter view, search and filters, and the dev-only `?display=` parameter is gone from a production build
* \[ \] With "Imported" selected in the Status filter in List, Board View and Tech View are greyed out in the switcher with the tooltip "Not available while Imported is selected." (S1-R13a)
* \[ \] In Board View and Tech View, "Imported" is greyed out in the Status filter with the tooltip "Imported work orders open in List." (S1-R13b)
* \[ \] Drag a card within one technician: order changes, no toast, no reassignment
* \[ \] A click on a row or card still opens the work order; a drag starts only after the pointer has moved a few pixels (S2-R8)
* \[ \] Drag a card to another technician: the lead changes, the toast appears, both counts update immediately, and lines follow the rules from Phase 3
* \[ \] Drag a card to Unassigned: the lead is removed
* \[ \] An Invoiced or Paid work order can be reordered within its technician (or within Unassigned) but not dropped on another technician, on Unassigned or out of it, and it shows that it can only be reordered (S4-N2); Declined and Complete move freely
* \[ \] Nothing can be dropped on an inactive technician
* \[ \] Cancelling a drag with Escape changes nothing
* \[ \] A failed move returns the card to where it started and shows the dismissible error
* \[ \] A reorder within one technician that can't be saved shows "Couldn't save the new order. Please try again." (S4-N10); a drop next to a card someone else has just moved shows "The card you dropped this next to has moved. Refresh the board and try again." (S4-N11); a second drop into the same technician at the same moment that can't be saved in time shows "The board is busy with another move. Please try again." (S4-N12); each time the card goes back to where it was
* \[ \] The shift prompt appears only when the outgoing lead has a work-order-level shift on this work order that hasn't ended (S4-R28), on both the drag and the dialog path, including a move to Unassigned (S4-R26); Keep and Clear behave as specified (Clear ends a shift under way at the moment of the change)
* \[ \] The prompt reads: title "Clear {Name}'s scheduled shifts?" (with no name: "Clear the lead technician's scheduled shifts?"); text "{Name} has shifts scheduled for this whole work order. Clearing removes shifts that haven't started and ends one that's under way now. Shifts on individual lines and recorded time don't change."; buttons "Keep shifts", "Clear shifts" and "Cancel" (S4-R29)
* \[ \] Cancel, or closing the prompt, calls the change off: a dragged card goes back to where it was; in the Reassign dialog I'm back in the dialog with my choice kept (S4-R30)
* \[ \] If clearing the shifts fails, the lead is not changed and the failure alert shows: the two succeed or fail together (S4-R27)
* \[ \] Changing the lead on the work order page or from the List shows no prompt (S4-R31)
* \[ \] Reordering technician columns works with view-only permission; dragging work orders needs create-and-edit
* \[ \] Pinned technicians reorder only among pins; Unassigned never moves
* \[ \] The work-order order survives reload, is the same in Tech View and Board View, and is the same for everyone at the location; technician order and pins stay per user
* \[ \] A new lead set from the Reassign dialog or the work order page puts the work order at the bottom of the new technician's work, for everyone; a dragged one lands where it is dropped; removing the lead from the Reassign dialog or the work order page puts it in Unassigned in the initial sort, not at the bottom; a work order created or split off with a lead keeps the initial sort (S9-R14)
* \[ \] Reordering with a filter on: the moved work order keeps its position relative to the visible neighbour when the filter is cleared
* \[ \] Below 1024px wide, on any device, the page shows the List as today with no display switcher, so the new displays and dragging are not offered; at 1024px and wider they are, including on a tablet held sideways (S1-R14)
* \[ \] Any bug found is fixed or raised as its own ticket before closing

### Done means

All verification tickets on this epic Done = the feature is ready for QA.

## SV-10231 — Verify Phase 12 — Performance validation at production scale

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10231 · Task · **TESTING QA** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Milan Zivanovic · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-09-18T06:18 · updated 2026-10-08T16:32 · resolution None
Links: relates to SV-10984; relates to SV-10985

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### What to verify

Phase 12 of the "Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options" technical plan. Ask Milan Zivanovic for the plan document.

Work Orders is the most-used page in the app, so the board must hold up at the size of the largest real shops: about 9,000 work orders in a location, up to 66 technicians, and around 8,200 of them with no lead technician.

Spec: [Work Orders — Board View & Tech View Display Options](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/~7120207b6068952672480db1a06bd810e23ba1/pages/845185030/Work+Orders+Board+View+Tech+View+Display+Options) · Epic: SV-10043

### Requirements covered

V-1, V-2, NFR-013

### Verification checklist

- [ ] The large-shop fixture seeds and the board loads against it
- [ ] Response times at the agreed targets: Work Orders view board ≤ 300 ms, All view ≤ 500 ms, All with search ≤ 700 ms, loading more ≤ 300 ms, the reassign dialog's technician list ≤ 100 ms (95th percentile)
- [ ] The List endpoint is no slower than its pre-change baseline plus 10%
- [ ] Query plans show no scan beyond the location's range, and the sort does not spill to disk
- [ ] The number of queries per request stays fixed no matter how many groups or cards are requested
- [ ] The board stays smooth while scrolling sideways across 66 columns, and dragging stays responsive
- [ ] A one-to-two-person shop is identified and tested on desktop: it looks right, including empty and overflow states (V-2)
- [ ] Both large-shop QA clones (Foothills Group, Haylock Truck and Trailer) behave as expected (V-1)
- [ ] Targets and results are recorded so QA and engineering can re-run them
- [ ] Any bug found is fixed or raised as its own ticket before closing

### Done means

All verification tickets on this epic Done = the feature is ready for QA.

## SV-10580 — POST /api/work-orders/lines/assign-technician has no permission check

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10580 · Task · **Board Backlog** · parent None · reporter Slavcho Mitrov · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-09-28T05:19 · updated 2026-10-14T03:34 · resolution None

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

**What's wrong:** the route has no `#[IsGranted]`, so any signed-in user at the location can assign a technician to a line through the API. The neighboring line-write routes require `ROLE_WORK_ORDER_CREATE_AND_EDIT`.

**Risk:** a user without edit permission can change line assignments with a direct request. Tenant scoping is fixed separately in SV-10043 Phase 3, so this is only about role.

**Where:** `Application/Line/AssignTechnician/AssignTechnicianController.php`

**Fix:** add `#[IsGranted(PermissionEnum::ROLE_WORK_ORDER_CREATE_AND_EDIT)]`. **First check for clients outside** `app/` (any mobile or time-clock client). If a technician-role flow calls this route, the permission has to allow it.

**Done when:**

* a user without the permission gets 403;
* existing app flows still work, covered by a functional test.

_Found in the SV-10043 Phase 3 backend review; land after Phase 3 merges._

### New comments since 7 Oct (1 of 1)
**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-14T03:34** (comment 78642)

Fixed on develop by [PR #3548](https://github.com/ShopView/shopview/pull/3548) (SV-10043, not merged yet), commit 5c816d26a9: assign-technician now has `#[IsGranted(ROLE_WORK_ORDER_CREATE_AND_EDIT)]`, covered by `LineTechnicianOrganizationCheckTest::testAssignTechnicianForbidsAUserWithoutWorkOrderEditPermission`.

Clients outside app/: every role that can view work orders holds this permission (the Work Orders view bundle grants it, Technician included), so a technician-role client keeps access. I'll close this when #3548 merges.


## SV-10583 — Work order task move/change endpoints write by client id without a tenant check

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10583 · Bug · **Open** · parent None · reporter Slavcho Mitrov · assignee None · created 2026-09-28T06:17 · updated 2026-10-14T03:34 · resolution None

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

**What's wrong:** the two task endpoints take task, line and technician ids from the request and use them without checking they belong to the caller's organization. On task move, a task id can reach a DELETE with no tenant predicate; on task change, tasks are loaded by line id with no tenant scope. On both, the technician id is written unchecked.

**Risk:** a signed-in user with work order edit rights could delete or rewrite another organization's work order task by id — and, through task change, that line's time, tech time and technician — or put a foreign technician on their own line. The code is identical on `main` and `develop`, so it is live in production.

**Affected endpoints:**

* `POST /api/work-orders/tasks/move`
* `POST /api/work-orders/tasks/change`

**Where:**

* `Application/Task/Move/MoveCommandHandler.php` — `tasks[].id` flows into `moveExistingTechTask()` (\~:111) → `TaskRepository::removeTaskByIds()`; `tasks[].tech_assigned_id` written at \~:65; lines resolved by organization only, not workplace; an unresolved old line dereferences null (500) at \~:72
* `Application/Task/Change/ChangeCommandHandler.php` — loads tasks with `TaskRepository::findBy(['lineId.value' => ...])`, unscoped; `tech_assigned_id` unchecked; `Domain/Line/ChangeLineSinceTaskChanged.php:22` looks the line up with an unscoped `findOneBy`
* `Infrastructure/Doctrine/Task/TaskRepository.php` — `removeTaskByIds()` (:126-135) deletes by id alone; `findBy` (:103-106) is unscoped

**Fix:** resolve every task and line through the caller's workplace and organization before any write (404 for anything not found), validate `tech_assigned_id` against the organization (fail closed, as `IsOrganizationStaffIdentifier` now does on the line paths), and give the repository's delete and lookup a tenant predicate. Decide the release line first: bugfix release on `main`, or the next regular release.

**Done when:**

* functional tests on both endpoints prove a foreign task, line or technician id is refused and nothing is deleted or written (fail before the fix);
* an unknown line returns 404 instead of 500.

_Found in the SV-10043 Phase 3 backend review (items C2, and C1 of the final re-review); pre-existing, left out of Phase 3 by decision._

### New comments since 7 Oct (1 of 1)
**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-14T03:34** (comment 78640)

Fixed on develop by [PR #3548](https://github.com/ShopView/shopview/pull/3548) (SV-10043, not merged yet), commits 5c816d26a9 and c81bc7f485:

* Both endpoints resolve every task and line through the caller's workplace and organization. Another organization's task or line is a 404, and nothing is written or deleted.
* `tech_assigned_id` must be staff of the caller's organization, and malformed ids are a 400.
* Tests: `ChangeTaskTenantCheckTest` (4) and `MoveTaskTenantCheckTest` (13).

Not done: `TaskRepository::removeTaskByIds()` and `findBy()` still have no tenant predicate (the handlers check before calling them), and main is unchanged. I'll close this when #3548 merges, unless we still want the repository-level predicate.

## SV-11074 — Board View – drag made while offline isn't reverted and is saved on reconnect

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11074 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10052 · reporter Ahtasham Amjad · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-08T16:48 · updated 2026-10-12T11:47 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Preconditions

* Role: Admin.
* A work order with no lead technician, sitting in the board's Unassigned column. I used S10043-17736 (ZZAUTOTEST F2 Tech Pin Order).

### Environment

* QA — sv10043.qa.shopview.com, build 7a95011, Chrome 154 / Windows 10.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Go to `https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders`.
2. Click the Board View icon (third in the group left of Create Work Order).
3. Search `S10043-17736` so only that card and the technician columns remain.
4. Open DevTools → Network tab → set the throttling dropdown to Offline.
5. Drag the card from Unassigned into any technician's column.
6. Wait 10 seconds. Watch for an alert and watch the card.
7. In the Network tab, confirm no `POST /api/work-orders/{id}/board-move` was sent.
8. Set throttling back to No throttling.
9. Reload the page and look at the work order's lead technician.

### Expected result

Per SV-10052 (S9-N2, applying S4-N3 and S4-N8) and TestRail C96968 and C97010, a move between technicians that cannot be saved is refused:

* The card returns to Unassigned.
* The dismissible alert "Failed to update lead technician, please try again." appears.
* The lead technician stays unchanged, including after the connection comes back.

### Actual result

No alert and no toast. The card stays in the new column. No request is sent while offline, so the server is still correct at step 7. On reconnect, though, the move is sent and saved. After the reload the work order has a lead technician it never had.

‌

‌

## SV-11087 — Lines tab – timesheet line change leaves technician on the source line

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11087 · Bug · **TESTING QA** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Ahtasham Amjad · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-09T07:14 · updated 2026-10-14T07:49 · resolution None
Links: relates to SV-10043; relates to SV-11249; relates to SV-11253; relates to SV-11277; relates to SV-11278

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Preconditions

* Role: Admin (Admin ShopView). Location: Staging Heavy Duty 9919.
* A work order with two lines, with Admin ShopView on neither. I used S10043-18489:

    * Line 1 "Service - Cabin air filter" — Labor: Esther Howard, Ralph Edwards
    * Line 2 "Replace - Suspension air bag" — Labor: Esther Howard, Ralph Edwards
    

### Environment

* QA — sv10043.qa.shopview.com, build 7a95011, Chrome 154 / Windows 10.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Open S10043-18489 → Lines tab.
2. On Line 1, click Start under Labor, wait \~60 seconds, click Stop, leave "Line Completed?" off, and click Clock Out.
3. Click ⋮ (top right of the lines table) → Timesheets, and open the new entry.
4. Change Line to "2 Replace - Suspension air bag" and click Save.
5. Hard-reload the Lines tab.

### Expected result

Per C368177, the entry and Admin ShopView move to Line 2. Line 1 keeps only Esther Howard and Ralph Edwards, as it does on staging and production today.

### Actual result

The hours move to Line 2 (Actual 0.02), and Admin ShopView appears there. Admin ShopView also stays on Line 1, with 0.00 hours and no timesheet entry behind it. The state persists after a hard reload.

‌

**Evidence:**

‌

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=e4d48d2a1169&id=8839bd5a-103e-441e-8bbc-62269fda1c22&&collection=&height=1410&occurrenceKey=null&width=1907&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=e47c266a3e0b&id=9dabc7b2-2181-45be-9d64-e8c2308a8f9d&&collection=&height=null&occurrenceKey=null&width=null&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
‌

### QA note

Regression from SV-10043: this doesn't happen on staging or production, only on QA (sv10043). It must be fixed before QA is merged to staging. Reproduced 3 of 3 times on two work orders. Moving the entry back leaves the technician on both lines, so every move adds a stray Labor row. Adding and removing a technician through Edit Line works correctly.

### New comments since 7 Oct (14 of 14)
**Chris Ward — 2026-10-09T07:54** (comment 78382)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Ahtasham Amjad</custom> should this be assigned inside the feature squad? Please re-home if so.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-09T08:31** (comment 78391)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Ahtasham Amjad</custom> thanks for the clear repro and the video. I traced it, and it isn't a regression from SV-10043. The same thing happens on staging and production today.

**Why it happens**

1. When a technician clicks Start on a line they aren't on, the app first adds a labor row for them to that line (SV-8766, live since v26.35.0).
2. When the timesheet entry's Line is changed, the app creates a labor row on the new line and moves the entry there, but nothing removes the row on the old line. So the old line keeps the technician with 0.00 h, and every move back and forth adds another row.

Every file involved (clock-in, the timesheet save, the timesheet line move, the Lines tab labor list and the Edit Timesheet dialog) is identical on production, develop and the SV-10043 branch.

**Why staging may not have shown it**

It only happens when the technician has no labor row on the source line before clocking in. If Admin ShopView was already on Line 1, or the line had an unassigned labor row, no extra row is created. Could you retry on staging with exactly the ticket's preconditions? I expect it to reproduce.

**Proposal**

* Drop the Regression label. This shouldn't block SV-10043 going to staging.
* I'll fix it on a separate branch: when an entry moves to another line, remove the technician's old labor row if they have no other time on that line.

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-1">@Chris Ward</custom> one product question: if the technician had been put on the line on purpose (through Edit Line) before logging time, should moving their entry also take them off that line? The fix as described would.

**Ahtasham Amjad — 2026-10-09T08:44** (comment 78395)

Yes <custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> , I could reproduce it on staging following your comment .  
  

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=4d70254e1963&id=8cb19450-16cd-4805-bddb-86056ee0cfc4&&collection=&height=855&occurrenceKey=null&width=1335&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
I’ve dropped the regression label, thanks

‌

cc: <custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-1">@Chris Ward</custom> 

**Chris Ward — 2026-10-09T08:56** (comment 78396)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> thanks for tracing it. Product call: no, keep deliberate assignments. When an entry moves to another line, only remove the labor row that clocking in added automatically (SV-8766), and only if the technician has no other time on that line. A technician someone put on the line on purpose (Edit Line) stays there, with 0.00 hours.

If the app can't tell an auto-added row from a deliberate one today, let me know before building and we'll decide again.

Agreed it's not a regression and shouldn't block SV-10043 going to staging.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-09T09:12** (comment 78397)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Chris Ward</custom> thanks. The app can't tell them apart today. A labor row only stores the line, the technician, its times and who created it, and the row added when someone clocks in looks exactly like one added through Edit Line. "Created by" doesn't help either: when Admin clocks in, or adds themselves through Edit Line, both rows say Admin.

Two options:

1. **Mark automatic rows from now on (my recommendation).** When clocking in, or moving a timesheet entry, adds a labor row, the row is flagged as automatic. Moving an entry then removes only a flagged row with no time left on its line; anyone added through Edit Line stays. Rows that already exist aren't flagged, so they count as deliberate and stay. Needs a small database change.
2. **Always remove the old row** when the technician has no time left on that line. Simpler, but it also removes deliberate assignments, which you've ruled out.

OK to build option 1?

If this isn't a priority right now, I can move it out of SV-10043 into the backlog instead.

**Chris Ward — 2026-10-09T11:11** (comment 78407)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> thanks, option 1 is approved. Mark the rows the app adds on its own (clock-in, entry moves) from now on. Moving an entry removes only a marked row that has no time left on its line. Rows that exist today count as deliberate and stay.

Please build it on its own branch, separate from SV-10043, so it doesn't hold up staging.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-12T02:50** (comment 78450)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Chris Ward</custom> one more case before I finish this. When a technician clicks Start on a line with nobody on it, the app doesn't add a row. It puts them on the line's empty labor row and makes them the line's technician. If their entry later moves to another line, the old line still shows them at 0.00 h and as its technician, the same symptom.

Should that line go back to unassigned when the entry moves away and they have no other time there? It's a small addition to the same fix. Until you answer, I'm building only what you approved.

Also, so you know: adding a timesheet entry by hand ("New timesheet") on a line where the technician has no labor row adds a row the same way, so I'm marking those too.

**Chris Ward — 2026-10-12T09:03** (comment 78508)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> yes, please include it. Same rule as before: undo only what the app did on its own. If clock-in filled the empty labor row and made them the line's technician, mark that, and when their time moves away and none is left there, the line goes back to Unassigned. If someone later assigned that technician on purpose (Edit Line), it stays.

Agreed on marking the rows a "New timesheet" entry adds too. Same branch, same fix.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-13T03:00** (comment 78555)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Chris Ward</custom> the claimed-row case is built and in code review: [PR #3619](https://github.com/ShopView/shopview/pull/3619), going into the bugfix release, separate from SV-10043.

**How it works**

* Labor rows the app adds on its own (Start, "New timesheet", a timesheet move) are marked. When the entry moves to another line and the technician has no time left there, the row is removed.
* When Start fills a line's empty labor row and makes them the line's technician, that's marked too. When their time moves away, the row and the line go back to Unassigned (unless another technician has a row there).
* Moving an entry onto a line whose only labor row is unassigned works like Start, even if that row holds the tech story. Moving away hands it back; the tech story stays.
* Anything done on purpose stays: a technician picked in Edit Line, a row someone wrote a tech story or estimate on, and every row from before this release.

**One rule to know**

Saving Edit Line without changing who is on the line doesn't count as on purpose, since the save re-sends everyone already there. So if only the description is edited, the line still goes back to Unassigned when the technician's time moves away. Say if you'd rather a plain save count.

**Not covered** (same as today; want tickets?)

* Deleting a timesheet entry, or changing it without a line move, leaves its labor row behind.
* A line with two or more unassigned labor rows is only partly cleaned up.



**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-13T06:54** (comment 78574)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Chris Ward</custom> one more for the "Not covered" list in my last comment, found in the PR's code review:

* Start fills a line's empty labor row for a technician, then a manager uses Move labour to line to move that row to another line. When the technician's time later moves off that second line, the row is handed back there as an empty, unassigned row, and the next technician who clocks in on that line gets it as an extra row. Hours and money aren't affected.

Same question as before: want a ticket for it?

**Chris Ward — 2026-10-13T08:13** (comment 78583)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> yes please, file it as a small Low bug for later, not in this PR. Hours and money aren't affected, so it can wait.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-13T08:29** (comment 78590)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Chris Ward</custom> thanks, filed as SV-11253. Same treatment for the two "Not covered" cases in my earlier comment (Low bugs for later)? And are you fine with a plain Edit Line save not counting as on purpose?

**Chris Ward — 2026-10-13T08:32** (comment 78592)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> yes to both:

1\. The two "not covered" cases (a deleted or changed entry leaving its labor row behind, and a line with two or more empty rows only partly cleaned up): please file them as Low bugs for later, same as SV-11253.

2\. A plain Edit Line save shouldn't count as on purpose. Only actually adding or changing a technician in Edit Line does. If the save just re-sends who's already on the line, the line still goes back to Unassigned when the app-filled technician's time moves away.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-14T07:39** (comment 78675)

SV-11087 is ready to test on https://sv11087.qa.shopview.com/ (build dc2c8a0). It ships in the bugfix release (PR #3619 into main), separately from SV-10043, so sv10043 doesn't have it.

Test with labor rows created on this env from now on. Rows that existed before the fix count as deliberate and are never removed.

1. The original repro: Start on Line 1 with a technician who has no labor row there, Stop, Clock Out. ⋮ → Timesheets → change the entry's Line to Line 2 → Save. Line 1 no longer lists the technician; Line 2 has the hours. Move it back: no extra rows on either line.
2. Add a technician to Line 1 through Edit Line, then repeat step 1 with them. They stay on Line 1 at 0.00 h, because they were added on purpose.
3. Start on an Unassigned line. The technician takes its empty labor row and becomes the line's technician. Move the entry to another line: the first line goes back to Unassigned.
4. Repeat step 3, but before moving, open Edit Line, change only the description and Save. The line still goes back to Unassigned (Chris's call above: a plain save doesn't count as on purpose).
5. Move an entry onto a line whose only labor row is unassigned and has a tech story. The technician takes that row and the tech story stays. Move it away: the row is unassigned again, tech story intact.
6. Repeat step 1 from Reports → Punch Clock Activities.

Not in this fix (Low bugs for later, same as production today):

* SV-11277: deleting an entry, or giving it to another technician, leaves its labor row.
* SV-11278: a line with two or more unassigned labor rows is only partly undone.
* SV-11253: a claimed row moved with Move labour to line comes back as a stray unassigned row.




## SV-11098 — Work Orders – "Assigned to me" Stays On After Changing Location

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11098 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10044 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-09T10:05 · updated 2026-10-12T08:33 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

When the user changes location, **"Assigned to me" should switch off**.

For example,

* a user turns on **Assigned to me** on the Work Orders page
* they change to another location from the menu under their initials
* the new location's Work Orders page opens with **Assigned to me still on**

It happens in **List**, **Tech View** and **Board View** alike.

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S1-E3).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Sign in and open **Work Orders**, then click the **All** tab.
2. Click **Board View** in the display switcher, then turn on **Assigned to me**.
3. Click your initials at the top right > **Change Location** and pick another location.

    * The top bar shows the new location.
    * **Assigned to me** is still on.
    
4. Click **Tech View**, turn on **Assigned to me**, and change back to the first location.

    * **Assigned to me** is still on.
    

**Actual Result:**

* After a location change, **Assigned to me** stays on in List, Tech View and Board View.

**Expected Result:**

* After a location change, **Assigned to me** is switched off.

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=ac348a2c-47d0-4aee-b91f-11b082779617&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Locations:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919, changed to ZZAUTOTEST Empty Shop · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Page:** [Work Orders](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders?tab=all)

## Sources

* [PRD – Work Orders Board View & Tech View](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) — S1-E3, read 8 Oct 2026:

    Assigned to me resets on location change, as it does today.



## Test case

* [C96923 Changing location resets the Work Orders page as it does today](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/96923)



### New comments since 7 Oct (4 of 4)
**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-12T04:44** (comment 78472)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Bilal Muzamil</custom> thanks. Production doesn't switch "Assigned to me" off on a location change either; it stays on, and the build keeps that behaviour. S1-E3 says "as it does today", so the sentence describes today's behaviour wrongly rather than the build being off. I've asked Chris to correct S1-E3, and C96923 needs the same change. No code change is planned for this ticket.

**Bilal Muzamil — 2026-10-12T04:47** (comment 78475)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Slavcho Mitrov</custom> Thanks Sir, you are right.  
<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-1">@Chris Ward</custom> Sir, once you update the PRD I will mark this test as passed.

**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-12T08:32** (comment 78499)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Bilal Muzamil</custom> Chris updated S1-E3 in the PRD (v45, 12 Oct): “Assigned to me stays as the user set it when the location changes, as it does today.” That's what the build does, so this one can be passed. SV-10044 and C96923 now say the same.

## SV-11099 — Work Orders – Search With No Results Has No "Clear filters" Action

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11099 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10044 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-09T10:10 · updated 2026-10-09T10:26 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10044

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

When a search finds no work orders, **the page should show the shared empty state with a Clear filters action in every display**.

For example,

* a search that matches nothing shows its own message instead of the shared one
* the message offers **no Clear filters** action
* the only way back is the small clear icon in the search box

When it is the **filters** (not the search) that find nothing, the page already shows the shared empty state with **Clear all filters**.

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S1-N1).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Sign in and open **Work Orders**, then click the **All** tab.
2. Click **Search** and type `ZZNOMATCH123`.

    * The page reads **No work orders match the search "ZZNOMATCH123".**
    * There is **no Clear filters** action.
    
3. Click **Tech View**, then **Board View** in the display switcher.

    * The same message shows in both, with no Clear filters action.
    

**Actual Result:**

* A search with no results shows **No work orders match the search "…"** with no Clear filters action, in List, Tech View and Board View.
* The same happens on a phone-width screen.

**Expected Result:**

* Every display shows **No work orders match your filters** with a **Clear filters** action.

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=8dfab22e-49c9-43ea-9381-62eaae1a346e&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Example record:** search text `ZZNOMATCH123`

## Sources

* [PRD – Work Orders Board View & Tech View](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) — S1-N1, read 8 Oct 2026:

    When filters return no results, show the existing List empty state and Clear filters action in every display: "No work orders match your filters".



## Test case

* [C368211 On a phone a no-match search shows the empty state with Clear filters](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368211)

## SV-11101 — Tech View & Board View – New Groups Don't Start in the List's Default Order

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11101 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10045 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-09T10:27 · updated 2026-10-09T10:52 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10045

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

Before anyone reorders a technician's work orders, **Tech View and Board View should list them in the List's default order** (customer name A-Z).

For example,

* one technician leads three work orders for customers whose names sort differently
* nobody has reordered that technician's work yet
* Tech View and Board View list them in the order they were created, not A-Z

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S2-R6).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Create three work orders in this order, for customers **Zeta Co**, **Alpha Co** and **Mid Co**, all with the same lead technician (one who leads nothing else).
2. Sign in as a user who has never reordered work, open **Work Orders** > **All** > **List**.

    * The three show as **Alpha Co, Mid Co, Zeta Co** (List's default, customer A-Z).
    * A user who has clicked a List column heading keeps that sort instead; the default is what a user who never changed it sees.
    
3. Click **Tech View** and find that technician's group; then click **Board View** and find that technician's column.

    * Both show **Zeta Co, Alpha Co, Mid Co** — the order they were created.
    

**Actual Result:**

* Before any reorder, a group/column lists its work orders in creation order.

**Expected Result:**

* Before any reorder, a group/column uses the List's default order: **Alpha Co, Mid Co, Zeta Co**.

* **Location:** Staging Lethbridge - 4310 · **User:** Carol Neal (her own Assigned to me)
* The List picture is set to **Customer** A-Z (the default) because this user had saved a different sort; the default itself was read as **ZZAUTOTEST WOB User B**, who never changed it.
* **Example records:** [S10043-17729](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders/1ca21752-35b5-4ebe-bfc2-446bf7bccf4f/lines) (ZZAUTOTEST Zeta Hauling, made first), [S10043-17730](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders/08b9a6a1-260d-495c-a4a7-aebe313ffc17/lines) (ZZAUTOTEST Alpha Freight), [S10043-17731](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders/40265ef6-cb8f-4ddc-a01e-24689b756986/lines) (ZZAUTOTEST Mid Trucking, made last), all led by Carol Neal

## Sources

## SV-11104 — Board View – "Card Has Moved" Alert Shows an Extra Headline

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11104 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10047 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-09T11:26 · updated 2026-10-12T04:53 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10047

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

**Please see the screen recording in the comment.**

---

When a card is dropped next to one that someone else has just moved, **the alert should read exactly the specified sentence**.

For example,

* two people have the same board open
* one moves a card away
* the other drops a card next to where it was
* the alert adds a general **Couldn't save the new order** headline above the sentence

The card itself goes back correctly.

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S4-N11).

## Steps to Reproduce (Intermittent)

1. Make three work orders led by one technician; open **Work Orders** > **Board View** in two browser tabs.
2. In tab 2, drag the middle card into another technician's column.
3. In tab 1, without refreshing, drop the last card directly above that middle card.

    * The alert reads **Couldn't save the new order. Please try again.** with **The card you dropped this next to has moved. Refresh the board and try again.** under it.
    

**Actual Result:**

* The alert shows two sentences: a general save-failed headline, then the specified sentence.

**Expected Result:**

* The alert reads only: **The card you dropped this next to has moved. Refresh the board and try again.**

## SV-11105 — Lines tab – deleting a scheduled shift adds an unrelated technician to the line

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11105 · Bug · **Code Review** · parent None · reporter Ahtasham Amjad · assignee Dusan Radulovic · created 2026-10-09T11:48 · updated 2026-10-12T11:36 · resolution None

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Preconditions

* Role: Admin (Admin ShopView). Location: Staging Heavy Duty 9919.
* A work order with a line, and a technician scheduled on it who is on no other line. I used S10043-18489:

    * Line 1 "Service - Cabin air filter" — Labor: Esther Howard, Admin ShopView, Ralph Edwards
    * Emily Madden scheduled on Line 1 via Schedule → Assign Work Order
    

### Environment

* QA — sv10043.qa.shopview.com, build 7a95011, Chrome 154 / Windows 10.
* Also reproduces on Staging, production.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Go to Schedule, double-click an empty slot on Emily Madden's row, choose Assign Work Order, select S10043-18489, pick Line 1 under "Choose lines", and create the shift.
2. Open S10043-18489 → Lines tab → Edit Line on Line 1. Emily Madden is listed with a calendar badge. Close with the ×.
3. Click Schedule in the top nav.
4. Click Emily Madden's shift block and delete it with the trash icon.
5. Press the browser Back button.
6. Open Edit Line on Line 1.

### Expected result

Per C368190, Line 1 lists only Esther Howard, Admin ShopView and Ralph Edwards.

### Actual result

Emily Madden is correctly gone, but a fourth technician appears — Cameron Johnston — who is on no line of this work order and was never scheduled on it. Closing and reopening the dialog clears the chip without a reload.

‌

**Evidence:**

‌  

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=71bab3f555cb&id=ede07539-3dad-4340-a6e1-b46ee59fcbd1&&collection=&height=null&occurrenceKey=null&width=null&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
### QA note

Not a regression from SV-10043 — also reproduces on production. Reproduced on the second of two attempts on sv10043. The phantom name appears tied to the scheduled technician rather than random: Emily Madden produced Cameron Johnston on two different work orders, which suggests a mis-resolved index into a technician list. The deleted shift's own technician does not persist, so the symptom differs from the original C368190 wording.
