## SV-11106 — Board View – Estimated Hours Never Shows on a Card

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11106 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10048 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-09T11:53 · updated 2026-10-13T02:21 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10048

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

When **Estimated hours** is turned on in **Fields to display**, **each card should show the work order's estimated hours**.

For example,

* a work order's lines carry estimated time
* Estimated hours is turned on
* the card shows every other chosen field, but no Estimated hours

In production a work order's estimated time is the sum of its lines' estimates.

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S5-R11).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open a work order whose lines have an **Estimated Time** (e.g. 0.3, 1.5 and 0.4 h).
2. Open **Work Orders** > **All** > **Board View** > **Fields to display**. Turn **Line count** on (if it is not on already) and turn **Estimated hours** on.
3. Click **Search**, type `ZZAUTOTEST F2 Card Field Values` and look at that work order's card.

    * The card shows the other chosen fields but **no Estimated hours**.
    * The same at **Compact**, **Regular** and **Comfortable** density.
    

**Actual Result:**

* No card shows Estimated hours, even when the work order's lines carry estimated time.

**Expected Result:**

* The card shows the work order's estimated hours (here **2.2**).

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=fd19f87a-e7d3-44ef-bf67-ca9394f30e1a&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)

* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Example record:** work order `S10043-18032` (customer `ZZAUTOTEST F2 Card Field Values 45183`), three lines estimated 0.30 + 1.50 + 0.40 h (Total Hours 2.2 hrs) — [S10043-18032](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders/49690594-84b3-4116-95a1-381fe5658688/lines)

## SV-11107 — Work Order Line – Adding a Second Technician Writes No History Entry

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11107 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10047 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-09T12:55 · updated 2026-10-09T13:15 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

A technician change made directly on a work order line **should be recorded in the line's history**.

For example,

* a line already has one technician (here the lead technician, who comes onto the line with the lead)
* a user opens the line in **Edit Line** and adds a second technician
* the change saves and both technicians show on the line
* the line's **Audit log** gets no entry for it

Adding the **first** technician to a line with none is recorded correctly (**Line tech changed**, **Tech: - → <name>**), so the gap is only when the line already has a technician.

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S4-R18).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Create a work order with one line and set its **Lead Technician** (e.g. **Ralph Edwards**). The lead also appears under the line's **Labor**.
2. On the line, click its menu > **Audit log**.

    * One entry: **Line created**.
    
3. Click the line to open **Edit Line**. Under **Technicians**, click **Add Technician**, pick a second technician (e.g. **Dana Ortiz**), then click **Save & Close**.

    * The line's **Labor** now lists **Ralph Edwards** and **Dana Ortiz**.
    
4. Open the line's menu > **Audit log** again.

    * Still one entry: **Line created**. Nothing records that Dana Ortiz was added.
    

**Actual Result:**

* Adding a technician to a line that already has one saves, but no entry is written to the line's history.
* The same with a third technician: adding **Esther Howard** to the same line (Ralph Edwards and Dana Ortiz already on it) also wrote nothing.

**Expected Result:**

* The technician change made on the line is recorded in the line's history, as it is when the first technician is added (**Line tech changed**).

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=3f8f4d36-913e-4b7f-8ca8-fabf63de38bb&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=5d6bfc87-58db-469b-ae2c-ab44e9c2abde&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Example record:** work order [S10043-18751](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders/414a9680-4159-49da-b134-6f16efb5ad27/lines) (customer `ZZAUTOTEST D8 Line History 13827`), Line 1 **Service - Cabin air filter**, lead Ralph Edwards, Dana Ortiz added 9 Oct 2026 17:25

## Sources

* [PRD – Work Orders Board View & Tech View](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) — S4-R18 (page version 44), read 9 Oct 2026:

    Continue existing line auditing for technician changes made directly on a work order line.

## SV-11117 — Board View – Field Choice After a Failed Settings Load Isn't Saved

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11117 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10048 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-10T02:19 · updated 2026-10-13T02:20 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10048

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

**See the screen recordings below.**

---

If a person's saved Work Orders settings fail to load, **the defaults should show and the person's next field choice should still be saved**.

For example,

* the saved settings do not load, so the defaults show (correct)
* the person turns a field on
* the field shows, but nothing is saved: after a reload it is gone

This appears inconsistent with the requirement defined in **Epic SV-10043** and the **PRD** (S5-N3).

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Have a developer make **only the loading** of the saved Work Orders choices fail (the `GET` of `/api/users/me/preferences/work-orders-list` returns an error). Saving must still work. _Do not use the browser's "Block request URL": it blocks the save as well._
2. Open **Work Orders** > **All** > **Board View**.

    * Board View shows its default fields (correct).
    
3. Click **Fields to display** and turn **On-site** on.

    * On-site shows on the cards.
    
4. Click Schedule → Click Workorders → Click ALL Click Board View Click Fields to display  
  and observe: 

    * **On-site** is off again: the choice was never saved.
    

**Actual Result:**

* The field choice made after the failed load is not saved; it is lost on reload.

**Expected Result:**

* The next field choice after a failed load is saved and is still there after the reload.

‌

* **Before Blocking**

    ```
      GET of /api/users/me/preferences/work-orders-list
    ```

    ![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=2bbe1f6284b6&id=05e2625f-4c0c-4dc4-87a6-2ad21eaeb262&&collection=&height=360&occurrenceKey=null&width=640&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)

    



    **After Blocking:**



    ```
      GET of /api/users/me/preferences/work-orders-list
    ```

     



     



    ![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=ca47e8b6a8b8&id=23bca4d9-59a0-4d10-9309-084b5543ae4a&&collection=&height=200&occurrenceKey=null&width=250&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)

‌

---

## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Example record:** any Work Orders page; the block is on the settings request only

## Sources

* [PRD – Work Orders Board View & Tech View](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) — S5-N2 and S5-N3, read 8 Oct 2026:

    If field preferences cannot load, use Tech View's starting columns (S5-R4) or Board View's default fields for the current display.



    Save the user's next field selection after a preference-load failure.



## Test case

* [C96983 If saved field choices can't load, the defaults show and the next change saves](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/96983)

## SV-11118 — Work Orders – Top Menu Keeps a Link's Tab and "Assigned to me" Instead of the Saved View

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11118 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10044 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-10T02:19 · updated 2026-10-12T01:13 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10044

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Do not pick this up - I need to double check it in the evening today.

---

After a Work Orders link has been opened, **clicking Work Orders in the top menu should show the user's own saved view**, not the link's.

For example,

* a link opens Work Orders on the **Estimates** tab with **Assigned to me** on
* the user then clicks **Work Orders** in the top menu
* the page stays on **Estimates** with **Assigned to me** on, instead of the saved **All** view

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open the link `https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders?tab=estimate&assigned_to_me=1`.

    * **Estimates** is selected and **Assigned to me** is on (as the link asks).
    
2. Click **Work Orders** in the top menu.

    * **Estimates** is still selected and **Assigned to me** is still on.

    * The saved **All** view shows, with **Assigned to me** off.
    

**Actual Result:**

* After a link, the top menu **Work Orders** keeps the link's tab and **Assigned to me** instead of the saved view.

**Expected Result:**

* Clicking **Work Orders** in the top menu shows the saved view (here **All**, **Assigned to me** off).

## SV-11119 — Board View – Two People Reordering the Same Technician: the Later Save Does Not Win

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11119 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10052 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-10T02:19 · updated 2026-10-12T01:08 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10052

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

When two people reorder the same technician's work orders at the same time, **the later save should win** and both should see that order after a refresh.

For example,

* two people have the same Board View open
* the first moves one card to the top of a technician's column
* the second, without refreshing, moves a different card to the top of the same column
* after a refresh both see a mix of the two moves, not the second person's order

## Steps to Reproduce

1. In two browsers open **Work Orders** > **All** > **Board View** and search the same customer; one technician leads three of its work orders, shown as **Alpha Co, Bravo Co, Charlie Co**.
2. In browser 1, drag **Charlie Co** to the top of that technician's column.

    * Browser 1 shows **Charlie Co, Alpha Co, Bravo Co**.
    
3. In browser 2, without refreshing, drag **Bravo Co** to the top of the same column.

    * Browser 2 shows **Bravo Co, Alpha Co, Charlie Co**. No warning in either browser.
    
4. Refresh both browsers.

    * Both show **Charlie Co, Bravo Co, Alpha Co**.
    

**Actual Result:**

* After both refresh, the column shows **Charlie Co, Bravo Co, Alpha Co** — a mix of the two moves.

**Expected Result:**

* The later save wins: both browsers show browser 2's order, **Bravo Co, Alpha Co, Charlie Co**.

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=5ee54b43-d7ec-47ce-8e85-7d9d1db1f543&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=9ad9af5d-6bb4-44e5-ba31-3ee847a58739&&collection=&height=1084&occurrenceKey=null&width=1962&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 · **Users:** two people signed in separately (Admin and the technician quick-login)
* **Example (10 Oct 2026, seen again):** customer search `ZZAUTOTEST F3 Later Drop Wins`, three work orders led by ZZAUTOTEST Ana Alpha: Alpha Co = S10043-18798, Bravo Co = S10043-18799, Charlie Co = S10043-18800 (the cards show the numbers)

## Sources

* [PRD – Work Orders Board View & Tech View](https://shopview.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/viewpage.action?pageId=845185030) — S9-R11 (page version 44), read 10 Oct 2026:

    Save the manual order of work orders once per location. Everyone at the location sees the same order, in both Tech View and Board View. If two people reorder at the same time, the later save wins; the other person sees the saved order on their next refresh and is not warned.

## SV-11126 — Board View – no "Clear shifts" prompt when outgoing lead has a work order shift

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11126 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10047 · reporter Ahtasham Amjad · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-10T16:21 · updated 2026-10-13T05:10 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Preconditions

* * Role: Admin.
  * A work order in Approved status with at least two lines and lead technician Technician A.
  * On the Schedule page, the work order has been dragged onto Technician A’s row and “Entire work order” chosen. The shift is today and hasn’t ended.
  

### Environment

* QA — sv10043.qa.shopview.com, Chrome 154 / Windows 10.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Open https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders, select Board View on the Work Orders tab, and search for the work order number.
2. In Technician A's column, open the card's More actions (…) menu and click "Reassign lead technician".
3. Select Technician B and click Reassign.

### Expected result

Per SV-10047 (S4-R11, S4-R28, S4-R31), a prompt appears before the change is saved:

* Title: "Clear Technician A's scheduled shifts?"
* Text: "Technician A has shifts scheduled for this whole work order. Clearing removes shifts that haven't started and ends one that's under way now. Shifts on individual lines and recorded time don't change." (S4-R29)
* Buttons: "Keep shifts", "Clear shifts" and "Cancel".

### Actual result

No prompt. The dialog closes and the toast "Lead technician updated" shows. Technician A's shift is still on the Schedule.

‌

**Evidence:**

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=8e27e5c1a117&id=398d551e-8757-4b34-a7b5-d1ed95ee6a08&&collection=&height=360&occurrenceKey=null&width=586&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
‌

### QA note

Reproduced 2 of 2.

‌

‌

**Test Case**

[https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368136](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368136)

### New comments since 7 Oct (2 of 2)
**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-12T09:47** (comment 78520)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Ahtasham Amjad</custom> Fixed on sv10043 (build `e84b3d1bc0`, PR #3548). A shift created with the Schedule's "Entire work order" option now counts as a whole-work-order shift. Changing the lead on Board View or Tech View brings up the "Clear …'s scheduled shifts?" prompt, and Clear shifts removes the shift. A shift on only some of the lines doesn't bring up the prompt. Chris wrote this into the PRD (S4-R28, v47).

**Bilal Muzamil — 2026-10-13T05:10** (comment 78568)

**QA Status: Passed:** [https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/tests/view/3761657](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/tests/view/3761657)


## SV-11128 — Board View – line technician doesn't follow new lead when shift scheduled

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11128 · Story Defect · **Done** · parent SV-10047 · reporter Ahtasham Amjad · assignee Slavcho Mitrov · created 2026-10-10T17:17 · updated 2026-10-13T05:11 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Preconditions

* Role: Admin.
* A work order in Approved status with one line, with no labor logged (Actual shows 0.00) and no one clocked in to it.
* Technician A is the work order's lead technician and got onto the line by being set as lead while the line was unassigned (an implicit assignment), not added through Edit Line.
* On the Schedule page, the work order has been dragged onto Technician B's row as **Entire work order**, for today at or after the current time marker, so the shift hasn't ended.

### Environment

* QA — sv10043.qa.shopview.com, Chrome 154.0.0.0 / Windows 10.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Open Work Orders, select Board View and search for the work order from the preconditions.
2. On its card in Technician A's column, open More actions and choose "Reassign lead technician".
3. Select Technician B and click Reassign.
4. Open the work order and go to the Lines tab.

### Expected result

Per SV-10047 (S4-R8) and TestRail C96960, the line's Labor technician changes to Technician B. The line is implicitly assigned to the outgoing lead, so it follows the new lead. None of the exceptions apply: no explicit assignment, no logged labor, no active clock-in.

### Actual result

Lead Technician shows Technician B, but the line's Labor technician is still Technician A.

### QA note

Reproduced 3 of 3. With no shift on the Schedule, the line follows the lead every time. Reassigning to a third technician who has no shift also leaves the line on Technician A. A shift that has already ended was not tested.

‌

**Evidence:**

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=f5c3db774b94&id=b914cd67-4e36-4875-81ee-22b04588468b&&collection=&height=1320&occurrenceKey=null&width=1907&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
‌

**Test Case:**

[https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/96960](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/96960)

### New comments since 7 Oct (2 of 2)
**Slavcho Mitrov — 2026-10-12T09:47** (comment 78521)

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-0">@Ahtasham Amjad</custom> Fixed on sv10043 (build `e84b3d1bc0`, PR #3548). When the lead changes, a line whose technician is there only because of a Schedule shift now follows the new lead, whether the shift was dropped on that line or on the entire work order. The shift technician stays on the line's technician list. Only a technician set through Edit Line keeps the line. Chris added this to the PRD (S4-R8 context note, v46).

**Bilal Muzamil — 2026-10-13T05:11** (comment 78569)

**→ QA Status: Passed**  
[https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/tests/view/3481904](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/tests/view/3481904)


## SV-11229 — Schedule – Dropped Shift Is Saved Six Hours After the Hour It Was Dropped On

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11229 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10228 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-12T03:41 · updated 2026-10-12T04:00 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

A shift made by dragging a work order onto the **Schedule** should start at the hour it is dropped on.

For example,

* dropped under **1 PM** on a technician's row, the shift is saved as **07:00 PM – 09:00 PM**
* dropped under **9 AM** as the whole work order, the shift is saved as **03:00 PM – 05:00 PM**

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Click **Schedule** in the top menu and click the **>** arrow next to **Today** to go to a working day.
2. In **Search work orders** (left) type a work order that has estimated hours left, and find its card.
3. Drag the card onto a technician's row and drop it under **1 PM**.
4. In the window, click **Choose lines**, tick one line, set **Hours** to **2h** and click **Create shift · 1 line · 2h**.

    * **Shift scheduled.** shows, with no error.
    
5. Reload the page, go to the same day and click the new shift on that row.

**Actual Result:**

* The shift's **TIME** reads **07:00 PM – 09:00 PM** and it is drawn under **7 PM**.

**Expected Result:**

* The shift reads **1:00 PM – 3:00 PM** and is drawn under **1 PM**, where it was dropped.

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=208dfeb6-a7ae-4020-9c0f-d9e0b0f65ded&&collection=&height=772&occurrenceKey=null&width=2548&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/schedule)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 (time zone America/Edmonton) · **User:** Admin (quick-login)
* **Examples:** S10043-19182 (one line, dropped under 1 PM, Fri Oct 16 2026 — seen again 12 Oct 2026, pictured above), S10043-18806 (one line, dropped under 1 PM, Mon Oct 12 2026) and S10043-18818 (whole work order, dropped under 9 AM, Tue Oct 20 2026), all on Esther Howard's row

## Sources

* Developer regression list (8 Oct 2026), High 3:

    Creating shifts by dragging onto the schedule should still work.


* For comparison, production (app.shopview.com, 12 Oct 2026) with its test location set to the same time zone (America/Edmonton): a shift dropped under 1 PM is saved **1:00 PM – 3:00 PM**, where it was dropped.

## Test case

* [C368179 Dragging a work order onto the Schedule with Choose lines makes a line shift](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368179)
* [C368178 Dragging a work order onto the Schedule as Entire work order makes a shift](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368178)

## SV-11230 — Timesheets – Recent Entry Listed Six Hours Ahead and Refused When Moved

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11230 · Story Defect · **OBSOLETE** · parent SV-10228 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-12T04:15 · updated 2026-10-12T04:44 · resolution Done

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

A time entry should be listed at the time it was made, and it should be possible to move it to another line of the same work order.

For example,

* an entry made at about **3:03 AM** shop time is listed as **09:03 AM – 09:05 AM**, six hours ahead
* moving that entry to another line is refused with **Start date cannot be set in the future. Please try to resolve this.**, and the **Timesheets** section stops loading

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open a work order that has two lines, click the **Lines** tab and click the clock button on **line 1** to clock in a technician. Wait a minute, click the clock button again and click **Clock Out**.
2. Click the three-dots button at the top right of the tabs and click **Timesheets**.

    * The new row's **Start Time** and **End Time** are six hours later than the shop's time.
    
3. Point at the row and click the pencil icon at its right. In the **Edit Timesheet** window, open **Line**, pick **line 2** and click **Save**.

    * The red message **Start date cannot be set in the future.** shows, and the section reads **Something went wrong loading this section**.
    
4. Click the **Lines** tab and reload the page.

**Actual Result:**

* The entry is listed six hours ahead of the shop's time.
* The move is refused as being in the future; line 2's **Labor** row still reads **Unassigned** and line 1 keeps the technician.

**Expected Result:**

* The entry is listed at the time it was made.
* The move saves with no error and line 2's **Labor** row shows the technician.

![](blob:https://media.staging.atl-paas.net/?type=file&localId=null&id=ec3cfdf3-f2a3-429d-b418-58a60a38ea1e&&collection=&height=568&occurrenceKey=null&width=1998&__contextId=null&__displayType=null&__external=false&__fileMimeType=null&__fileName=null&__fileSize=null&__mediaTraceId=null&url=null)
## Environment

* **Build:** `v26.40.8-7a95011` on the QA branch [sv10043](https://sv10043.qa.shopview.com/workorders)
* **Location:** Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 (time zone America/Edmonton) · **User:** Admin (quick-login), clocking in as Esther Howard
* **Examples:** S10043-19183 (12 Oct 2026, entry listed 09:03 AM – 09:05 AM at about 3:03 AM shop time) and S10043-18865 (10 Oct 2026)

## Sources

* Developer regression list (8 Oct 2026), High 2:

    Move a labor task from one line to another: the target line takes the technician.


* For comparison, production (app.shopview.com, 12 Oct 2026) with its test location set to the same time zone (America/Edmonton): an entry made at 02:49 AM is listed **02:49 AM – 02:50 AM** and moves to line 2 after **Confirm**, with no refusal.

## Test case

* [C368177 Moving labor to another line gives that line the technician](https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/368177)

## SV-11235 — Work order page: Lead Technician shows blank instead of "Unassigned" when the work order has no lead

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11235 · Bug · **Ready for Production** · parent None · reporter Chris Ward · assignee Dusan Radulovic · created 2026-10-12T08:40 · updated 2026-10-14T06:12 · resolution None
Links: relates to SV-10043

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### Summary

On the work order page, the Lead Technician field is blank whenever the work order opens with no lead technician. It should read "Unassigned", the same as right after someone picks Unassigned, and the same as Service Advisor and Sales Representative.

### Steps to reproduce

1. Open a work order that has a lead technician and lines.
2. In Lead Technician, pick "Unassigned". The field reads "Unassigned".
3. Reload the page.

### Expected

Lead Technician reads "Unassigned".

### Actual

Lead Technician is blank. Any work order with no lead opens this way.

### Notes

Found while answering a Board View / Tech View QA question (SV-10043). It happens on production today, so it is not caused by Board View / Tech View and should not block it. Checked on a production build: right after picking Unassigned the field reads "Unassigned"; after a reload it is blank.

## SV-11240 — Work Orders Analytics – Report Checks Blocked Without Google Analytics Access

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11240 · Task · **Done** · parent SV-10043 · reporter Bilal Muzamil · assignee None · created 2026-10-12T12:16 · updated 2026-10-12T12:33 · resolution Done
Links: relates to SV-10593

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

## Description

Three test cases for the Work Orders analytics story (<custom data-type="smartlink" data-id="id-0">https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-10593#icft=SV-10593</custom>) can only be judged by reading the **Story 12 reports in Google Analytics**. QA has no access to Google Analytics, so these three checks are **blocked**. Everything else in the story is tested: the events the Work Orders page sends were checked and **passed**.

<custom data-type="mention" data-id="id-1">@Stefan Mitrovic</custom>, could you please let us know which of these applies?

* **These three checks can be set aside for now.** We will close them for this release and pick them up when the reports are available.
* **Or QA can be given a way to read the reports.** For example, read-only (Viewer) access to the Google Analytics property the QA and staging builds send to (`G-CMYCXL13C9`) for `[email removed]`, or the name of the person who can read the three reports for our test dates.

## SV-11279 — change-service-advisor and change-customer accept another organization's ids

https://shopview.atlassian.net/browse/SV-11279 · Bug · **Open** · parent None · reporter Slavcho Mitrov · assignee None · created 2026-10-14T02:56 · updated 2026-10-14T08:03 · resolution None
Links: relates to SV-10043; relates to SV-11249; relates to SV-11198; relates to SV-10242

### Description (new to us (not in the 8 Oct capture))

### What's broken

* `POST /api/work-orders/change-service-advisor` loads the work order by id alone, so a user can change another organization's work order (an unknown id gives a 500). It also saves any staff id as the service advisor without checking that it belongs to the caller's organization. Develop's SV-10242 already notes it "validates ownership of neither id".
* `POST /api/work-orders/change-customer` resolves `customer_id` with a bare lookup and never checks the contact's organization (creating a work order does). A contact from another organization can be saved on your own work order.

### Risk

A cross-tenant write, and a read leak through it. Once another organization's contact is saved on a work order, the schedule sidebar card prints that contact's first and last name, and List/Board search returns the work order only while a guess matches the contact's name or email, so repeated searches spell it out. SV-10043 already hides other organizations' staff names on the List and Board; contacts aren't covered. The ids are UUIDs, so it needs a leaked id.

### Where in code

* change-service-advisor handler: work order loaded unscoped; advisor id not checked.
* `ChangeCustomer/ChangeCommand`: `#[MapEntity]` → `CustomerRepository::findById` (bare find); compare `CreateCommand`, which checks the organization.
* Reads: `WorkOrderListingCriteriaApplier` joins the contact (`cus`) on `wo.customer_id` only and searches its name and email (List and Board share it); `DbalSidebarWorkOrderFetcher` joins it the same way and returns the contact's names. The company and vehicle joins carry no tenant predicate either, though no current writer stores another organization's id there.
* `WorkOrderListingCriteriaApplier` arrives with PR #3548 (SV-10043). Until that merges, the same List join and search are in `Application/List/ListingQueryHandler.php` (:477) on develop and production.

### Fix

* change-service-advisor: scope the work-order lookup with both decorators (404 when unknown or foreign). After the same-id short-circuit, check the advisor with `StaffRepository::findByIdInOrganization()` (422, mirroring `LeadTechnicianNotInOrganizationError`).
* change-customer: check the contact's organization the way `CreateCommand` does.
* Reads, as defence in depth: join the contact only within the work order's own company (`cus.company_id = wo.company_id`, as `WorkOrderDetailsFetcher` does) in the listing criteria and the sidebar. Optionally add organization predicates to the company and vehicle joins.

### Done when

* Both endpoints refuse a foreign or unknown work order, staff member or contact with a 4xx, never a 500.
* A work order that already holds another organization's contact shows no contact name in the sidebar and isn't matched by searching that contact's name or email.
* Functional cross-organization tests cover both writers and both reads.

_Found in the PR #3548 self-review (SR-17 and its round-3 triage). Already on develop and in production; not caused by SV-10043._
